📚 本期文献进展 / Literature Brief

  • 本期SARS-CoV-2研究集中呈现以下进展。
  • 分析显示,尽管病毒持续多样化,Mpro 的 进化 在最普遍的 严重急性呼吸综合征冠状病毒2 变异株中仍保持显著的限制,其活性位点和二聚化界面区域的氨基酸替换非常有限。 [10.1002/prot.70183]。
  • GSK-3抑制被发现可延迟子代病毒从被感染细胞中释放,且严重急性呼吸综合征冠状病毒2变体中的NR203M和NR204R 突变减少了GSK-3介导的磷酸化效率。 [10.1111/bph.70679]。
  • 联合接种这两种B/HPIV3 媒介扩大了S特异性抗体反应的广度,覆盖了每种媒介单独接种所诱导的反应范围。接种混合疫苗的仓鼠对祖先和近期流行的严重急性呼吸综合征冠状病毒2株均产生了中和抗体,并在接种后5个月对BA.5/Omicron挑战表现出保护作用。
  • 在研究 I 中,77% 的 CLL 患者需要住院治疗,24% 需要重症监护,大约 30% 死亡。在研究 II 中,60% 的 CLL 患者出现血清转化,抗体滴度较低,而 29% 的患者观察到 T 细胞反应。在研究 IV 中,94% 的 CLL 患者接受了两剂 疫苗,88% 接受了三剂,78% 接受了四剂,56% 接受了五剂 疫苗。在研究 V 中,与对照组相比,CLL 患者 90 天全因 死亡率 的调整后风险比为 1.70,年轻患者和某些 治疗 患者的风险较高。

  • The analysis showed that despite ongoing viral diversification, Mpro evolution has remained remarkably constrained, with limited amino-acid substitutions in the active-site and dimerization-interface regions across the most prevalent SARS-CoV-2 variants.
  • GSK-3 inhibition was found to delay the release of progeny virus from infected cells, and the NR203M and NR204R mutations in SARS-CoV-2 variants reduced the efficiency of GSK-3-mediated phosphorylation.

Journal: Yonsei medical journal  |  Canonical publication date: 2026-10-01  |  DOI: 10.3349/ymj.2025.0533  |  Date basis: first_publication_date  |  Publication status: Future scheduled publication  |  Citation: 2026, 67, 10, 724-735

Incidence and Outcomes of Bloodstream Infections in Critically Ill Patients with COVID-19: A Nationwide Multicenter Study in South Korea.

Incidence and Outcomes of Bloodstream Infections in Critically Ill Patients with COVID-19: A Nationwide Multicenter Study in South Korea.
作者： Ah Young Leem, Shihwan Chang, Min-Dong Sung, Chan-Ho Lee, Kyung Soo Chung, Young Sam Kim, Sunghoon Park, Onyu Park, Taehwa Kim, Hye Ju Yeo
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
Coronavirus disease 2019 (COVID-19) increases the risk of bloodstream infections (BSI), but associated risk factors remain incompletely characterized. We aimed to evaluate BSI risk factors in patients admitted to the intensive care unit for COVID-19.
设计与对象
This nationwide multicenter study, conducted across 22 university-affiliated hospitals in South Korea, included COVID-19 patients treated with high-flow nasal cannula (HFNC) therapy or mechanical ventilation.
核心方法
对病原体进行了分析,采用多变量逻辑回归评估危险因素,并进行了补充的Cox比例风险分析以处理时间依赖性结局。
主要结果
纳入的573名患者中,206名(35.9%)至少发生了一次BSI。金黄色葡萄球菌属是最常见的分离菌种。随着时间推移,革兰氏阴性菌的检出率逐渐上升,而真菌感染则与住院时间延长相关。
解释与创新
多变量分析确定慢性神经系统疾病、较高的基线序贯器官衰竭评估(SOFA)评分、机械通气、俯卧位通气以及感染前使用体外膜肺氧合(ECMO)为独立的BSI预测因素。值得注意的是,尽管BSI组的院内死亡率粗死亡率显著更高(41.5% vs. 31.7%,p=0.012),但在逻辑回归或Cox分析中,BSI并不是院内死亡率的独立预测因素。
科研与公卫意义
In critically ill patients with COVID-19, high baseline severity, chronic neurological disease, and pre-infection advanced life support (mechanical ventilation and ECMO) were independent predictors of BSI. Rather than acting as an independent cause of death, BSI primarily serves as a clinical marker of extreme illness severity and prolonged invasive interventions.
局限与证据强度
所提供的证据未报告该研究的局限性。

Incidence and Outcomes of Bloodstream Infections in Critically Ill Patients with COVID-19: A Nationwide Multicenter Study in South Korea.

Authors: Ah Young Leem, Shihwan Chang, Min-Dong Sung, Chan-Ho Lee, Kyung Soo Chung, Young Sam Kim, Sunghoon Park, Onyu Park, Taehwa Kim, Hye Ju Yeo
Original abstract (English)
PURPOSE: Coronavirus disease 2019 (COVID-19) increases the risk of bloodstream infections (BSI), but associated risk factors remain incompletely characterized. We aimed to evaluate BSI risk factors in patients admitted to the intensive care unit for COVID-19. MATERIALS AND METHODS: This nationwide multicenter study, conducted across 22 university-affiliated hospitals in South Korea, included COVID-19 patients treated with high-flow nasal cannula (HFNC) therapy or mechanical ventilation. Baseline characteristics were compared between patients who developed BSI within 30 days and those who did not. Pathogens were analyzed, risk factors were evaluated using multivariable logistic regression, and supplementary Cox proportional hazards analyses were performed to address time-dependent outcomes. RESULTS: Of 573 included patients, 206 (35.9%) developed at least one BSI episode. Staphylococcus species were the most frequent isolates. Gram-negative bacteria became increasingly prevalent over time, while fungal infections were associated with prolonged hospitalization. Multivariable analysis identified chronic neurological disease, higher baseline Sequential Organ Failure Assessment score, mechanical ventilation, prone positioning, and extracorporeal membrane oxygenation (ECMO) use prior to infection as independent BSI predictors. Notably, although crude in-hospital mortality was significantly higher in the BSI group (41.5% vs. 31.7%, p=0.012), BSI was not an independent predictor of in-hospital mortality in either logistic regression or Cox analyses. CONCLUSION: In critically ill patients with COVID-19, high baseline severity, chronic neurological disease, and pre-infection advanced life support (mechanical ventilation and ECMO) were independent predictors of BSI. Rather than acting as an independent cause of death, BSI primarily serves as a clinical marker of extreme illness severity and prolonged invasive interventions.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Coronavirus disease 2019 (COVID-19) increases the risk of bloodstream infections (BSI), but associated risk factors remain incompletely characterized. We aimed to evaluate BSI risk factors in patients admitted to the intensive care unit for COVID-19.
Design and population
This nationwide multicenter study, conducted across 22 university-affiliated hospitals in South Korea, included COVID-19 patients treated with high-flow nasal cannula (HFNC) therapy or mechanical ventilation.
Core methods
Pathogens were analyzed, risk factors were evaluated using multivariable logistic regression, and supplementary Cox proportional hazards analyses were performed to address time-dependent outcomes.
Main results
Of 573 included patients, 206 (35.9%) developed at least one BSI episode. Staphylococcus species were the most frequent isolates. Gram-negative bacteria became increasingly prevalent over time, while fungal infections were associated with prolonged hospitalization.
Interpretation and novelty
Multivariable analysis identified chronic neurological disease, higher baseline Sequential Organ Failure Assessment score, mechanical ventilation, prone positioning, and extracorporeal membrane oxygenation (ECMO) use prior to infection as independent BSI predictors. Notably, although crude in-hospital mortality was significantly higher in the BSI group (41.5% vs. 31.7%, p=0.012), BSI was not an independent predictor of in-hospital mortality in either logistic regression or Cox analyses.
Scientific and public-health significance
In critically ill patients with COVID-19, high baseline severity, chronic neurological disease, and pre-infection advanced life support (mechanical ventilation and ECMO) were independent predictors of BSI. Rather than acting as an independent cause of death, BSI primarily serves as a clinical marker of extreme illness severity and prolonged invasive interventions.
Limitations and evidence strength
The supplied evidence does not report limitations of the study.

严重急性呼吸综合征冠状病毒2 主蛋白酶在折叠稳定性与活性约束下的进化限制

Evolution of the SARS ‐ CoV ‐2 Main Protease Under Fitness Constraints Imposed by Folding Stability and Activity
作者： David Ferreiro, Miguel Arenas
摘要中文翻译摘要 严重急性呼吸综合征冠状病毒2(严重急性呼吸综合征冠状病毒2)持续进化和多样化,导致免疫逃逸变异株的出现,凸显了需要针对保守病毒机制的互补性 抗病毒药物 策略的重要性。在此背景下,主蛋白酶(Mpro)因其在病毒复制中的核心作用以及与人类蛋白酶低相似性,代表了一个潜在的治疗靶点。然而,Mpro 变异对底物识别和潜在抗性的影响仍缺乏充分研究。通过对多种 严重急性呼吸综合征冠状病毒2 Mpro 变异株的分析,我们发现,尽管病毒持续多样化,自2022年与奥密克戎相关的P132H谱系出现以来,Mpro 的 进化 保持了显著的限制,其活性位点和二聚化界面区域的氨基酸替换非常有限。在最普遍的 严重急性呼吸综合征冠状病毒2 变异株中,Mpro 对底物的整体识别基本保持不变,尽管某些变异株与底物的配对在每个残基的相互作用和氢键模式上表现出一定的异质性。集合动力学分析揭示了某些 Mpro 变异株中保守的大尺度运动,以及在某些情况下与底物相关的次要运动的适度变化。总体而言,严重急性呼吸综合征冠状病毒2 主蛋白酶的 进化 强烈受到结构和功能需求的限制,支持其作为有效 抗病毒药物 靶点的适用性。研究结果进一步表明,考虑病毒的进化动态可能有助于设计更具长期稳健性的治疗策略。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
本研究旨在探讨由于结构和功能需求导致的 严重急性呼吸综合征冠状病毒2 主蛋白酶(Mpro)的进化限制,强调了需要针对保守病毒机制的互补性 抗病毒药物 策略的重要性。
设计与对象
本研究分析了多种 严重急性呼吸综合征冠状病毒2 主蛋白酶(Mpro)变异株,重点关注自2022年与奥密克戎相关的P132H谱系出现以来的 进化,并考察了其在普遍变异株中的结构和功能特征。
核心方法
研究人员通过集合动力学分析多种 严重急性呼吸综合征冠状病毒2 主蛋白酶(Mpro)变异株,评估其大尺度运动和与底物相关的次要运动变化,同时分析不同变异株之间的底物识别和相互作用。
主要结果
分析显示,尽管病毒持续多样化,Mpro 的 进化 在最普遍的 严重急性呼吸综合征冠状病毒2 变异株中仍保持显著的限制,其活性位点和二聚化界面区域的氨基酸替换非常有限。
解释与创新
作者认为,Mpro 的 进化 强烈受到结构和功能需求的限制,这支持其作为有效 抗病毒药物 靶点的适用性,并建议将病毒的进化动态纳入考虑,以提高治疗策略的稳健性。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现可能通过考虑其进化动力学,为设计更具鲁棒性的针对严重急性呼吸综合征冠状病毒2的治疗策略提供信息,从而增强长期抗病毒药物的疗效。
局限与证据强度
本研究的结果基于对多种变异株的分析,但证据仅限于结构和功能观察,未报告直接的临床或流行病学结果。

Evolution of the SARS ‐ CoV ‐2 Main Protease Under Fitness Constraints Imposed by Folding Stability and Activity

Authors: David Ferreiro, Miguel Arenas
Original abstract (English)
ABSTRACT The severe acute respiratory syndrome coronavirus 2 (SARS‐CoV‐2) continues to evolve and diversify, leading to the emergence of immune‐evasive variants and underscoring the need for complementary antiviral strategies targeting conserved viral machinery. In this context, the main protease (Mpro) represents a potential therapeutic target because of its central role in viral replication and its low similarity to human proteases. However, the evolutionary consequences of Mpro variation on substrate recognition and potential resistance remain insufficiently explored. Through the analysis of multiple SARS‐CoV‐2 Mpro variants, we show that, despite ongoing viral diversification, Mpro evolution has remained remarkably constrained since the emergence of the Omicron‐associated P132H lineage in 2022, with limited amino‐acid substitutions in the active‐site and dimerization‐interface regions. Across the most prevalent SARS‐CoV‐2 variants, overall substrate recognition by Mpro was largely preserved, although specific variant‐substrate pairs exhibited some heterogeneity in per‐residue interactions and hydrogen‐bond patterns. Ensemble dynamics revealed conserved large‐scale motions alongside modest substrate‐dependent shifts in secondary motions in certain Mpro variants. Overall, SARS‐CoV‐2 Mpro evolution is strongly constrained by structural and functional requirements, supporting the suitability of this protein as an effective antiviral target. The findings further suggest that incorporating viral evolutionary dynamics may help inform the design of therapeutic strategies with improved long‐term robustness.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to investigate the evolutionary constraints on the SARS-CoV-2 main protease (Mpro) due to its structural and functional requirements, highlighting the need for complementary antiviral strategies targeting conserved viral machinery.
Design and population
The study analyzed multiple SARS-CoV-2 Mpro variants, focusing on their evolution since the emergence of the Omicron-associated P132H lineage in 2022, and examined their structural and functional characteristics across prevalent variants.
Core methods
The researchers conducted an analysis of SARS-CoV-2 Mpro variants using ensemble dynamics to assess large-scale motions and substrate-dependent shifts in secondary motions, while also evaluating substrate recognition and interactions across variants.
Main results
The analysis showed that despite ongoing viral diversification, Mpro evolution has remained remarkably constrained, with limited amino-acid substitutions in the active-site and dimerization-interface regions across the most prevalent SARS-CoV-2 variants.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpreted that Mpro evolution is strongly constrained by structural and functional requirements, which supports its use as an effective antiviral target, and suggest that incorporating viral evolutionary dynamics could improve the robustness of therapeutic strategies.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings may inform the design of more robust therapeutic strategies against SARS-CoV-2 by considering its evolutionary dynamics, which could enhance long-term antiviral efficacy.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study's results are based on analysis of multiple variants, but the evidence is limited to structural and functional observations, with no direct clinical or epidemiological outcomes reported.

重新定位尼氯霉素和阿来替尼以抑制严重急性呼吸综合征冠状病毒2核衣壳蛋白的过度磷酸化

Repositioning niclosamide and alectinib to inhibit the hyperphosphorylation of SARS‐CoV‐2's nucleocapsid protein
作者： Song Chen, Zu-Ping He, Jean E. Kanyo, TuKiet T. Lam, Grace Ha, Brett D. Lindenbach, Ya Ha, 哈亚
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
本研究旨在解决抑制严重急性呼吸综合征冠状病毒2的核衣壳蛋白过度磷酸化的问题,这一过程对病毒复制和基因组RNA解包至关重要。
设计与对象
该研究在Calu-3和Vero E6细胞中进行,以探讨GSK-3抑制对严重急性呼吸综合征冠状病毒2复制的影响,重点关注病毒的核衣壳蛋白及其磷酸化动态。
核心方法
研究人员使用液相色谱-串联质谱技术评估了纯化N蛋白的磷酸化谱型,并在表达病毒蛋白的293T细胞中检查了GSK-3抑制剂的剂量依赖效应。
主要结果
GSK-3抑制被发现可延迟子代病毒从被感染细胞中释放,且严重急性呼吸综合征冠状病毒2变体中的NR203M和NR204R 突变减少了GSK-3介导的磷酸化效率。
解释与创新
作者将他们的发现解释为一种新型药理学方法的证据,该方法结合尼氯胺和阿来替尼部分降解GSK-3并恢复对已临床测试的抑制剂恩扎斯塔林的敏感性。
科研与公卫意义
The findings suggest a potential new strategy for treating COVID-19 by targeting the phosphorylation of SARS-CoV-2's nucleocapsid protein through the repositioning of existing drugs.
局限与证据强度
该研究的证据仅限于体外实验,观察到的对病毒释放的影响可能无法完全适用于体内环境,因此得出中等置信度的评估。

Repositioning niclosamide and alectinib to inhibit the hyperphosphorylation of SARS‐CoV‐2's nucleocapsid protein

Authors: Song Chen, Zu-Ping He, Jean E. Kanyo, TuKiet T. Lam, Grace Ha, Brett D. Lindenbach, Ya Ha, 哈亚
Original abstract (English)
BACKGROUND AND PURPOSE: In the past two decades, three pathogenic coronaviruses, including SARS-CoV-2, spilled over from natural animal reservoirs into the human population. Upon entry into infected cells, the nucleocapsid (N) protein of coronavirus becomes heavily phosphorylated within its central Ser-Arg domain by host kinase GSK-3, which triggers genomic RNA unpackaging and facilitates its recruitment to the replication transcription complex. Although initially considered a promising antiviral strategy, inhibiting N hyperphosphorylation requires high concentrations of kinase inhibitor and is impractical to achieve by conventional pharmacological means. EXPERIMENTAL APPROACH: We investigated the effect of GSK-3 inhibition on SARS-CoV-2 replication in Calu-3 and Vero E6 cells. The dose-dependent effect of the GSK-3 inhibitor to block N hyperphosphorylation was examined in 293T cells heterologously expressing the viral protein. Liquid chromatography-tandem mass spectrometry analysis was performed to evaluate the phosphorylation profile of the purified N protein. KEY RESULTS: GSK-3 inhibition delays the release of progeny virus from infected cells. N hyperphosphorylation relies on a complex, redundant priming mechanism and exhibits strong cooperativity, which together contribute to resistance against GSK-3 inhibitors. The NR203M and NR203K/G204R mutations-present in the delta and omicron variants of concern (VOCs), respectively-reduce the efficiency of GSK-3-mediated phosphorylation. We discovered a novel pharmacological tool combining niclosamide and alectinib that partially degrades GSK-3 and restores the sensitivity of N hyperphosphorylation to the clinically tested GSK-3 inhibitor, enzastaurin. CONCLUSIONS AND IMPLICATIONS: This work provides the biochemical basis for a novel approach to treat COVID-19 by synergistically repositioning two FDA-approved drugs.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to address the challenge of inhibiting SARS-CoV-2's nucleocapsid protein hyperphosphorylation, a process critical for viral replication and genomic RNA unpackaging.
Design and population
The study was conducted in Calu-3 and Vero E6 cells to investigate the effect of GSK-3 inhibition on SARS-CoV-2 replication, with a focus on the virus's nucleocapsid protein and its phosphorylation dynamics.
Core methods
The researchers used liquid chromatography-tandem mass spectrometry to evaluate the phosphorylation profile of the purified N protein and examined the dose-dependent effect of GSK-3 inhibitors in 293T cells expressing the viral protein.
Main results
GSK-3 inhibition was found to delay the release of progeny virus from infected cells, and the NR203M and NR204R mutations in SARS-CoV-2 variants reduced the efficiency of GSK-3-mediated phosphorylation.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpreted their findings as evidence for a novel pharmacological approach that combines niclosamide and alectinib to partially degrade GSK-3 and restore sensitivity to the clinically tested inhibitor enzastaurin.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings suggest a potential new strategy for treating COVID-19 by targeting the phosphorylation of SARS-CoV-2's nucleocapsid protein through the repositioning of existing drugs.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study's evidence is limited to in vitro experiments, and the observed effects on viral release may not fully translate to in vivo settings, resulting in a moderate confidence assessment.

Journal: International Journal of Contemporary Pediatrics  |  Canonical publication date: 2026-09-23  |  DOI: 10.18203/2349-3291.ijcp20263317  |  Date basis: online_date  |  Publication status: Current window  |  Citation: 2026

长新冠在儿科人群中的临床表现、演变及管理:从早期大流行到当前时代的一项系统综述

Long COVID in the pediatric population – clinical evolution, prevalence and management from the early pandemic to the current era: a systematic review
作者： Paquita R. Q. Donoso, Jaime V. M. Muñoz, Joba E. I. Imbaquingo, Luis A. R. Vásconez, Eliana M. C. Peñafiel, Verónica J. M. Cuenca
摘要中文翻译新型冠状病毒感染后急性后遗症(PASC),即长新冠,在儿科人群中呈现出复杂的临床图景,其演变从最初的疫情浪潮发展到以奥密克戎变种为主导、广泛传播的当前时代。理解其演变、临床轨迹及基于证据的治疗策略对于儿科医疗保健至关重要。本研究的目的是系统评估从早期大流行到当前时代,儿科患者长新冠的临床表现、演变率变化、主要症状群及管理策略。系统综述了2020年至2026年间在PubMed、Scopus、Cochrane Library和Embase上发表的文献。根据PRISMA指南,纳入了观察性研究、前瞻性队列分析以及评估儿童和青少年(0-18岁)感染后12周及以上急性后症状的临床试验数据。报告的儿科长新冠患病率在疫情各阶段显著变化,从奥密克戎变种之前的10-25%下降到当前时代的2-8%,这主要受到儿科新冠疫苗接种覆盖率和先前混合免疫的影响。最持久的临床表现从系统性疲劳、头痛和呼吸困难转变为持续性疲劳、神经认知功能障碍("脑雾")、情绪障碍和自主神经功能障碍(如POTS样症状)。管理策略仍主要以多学科和症状针对性为主,侧重于定制化的身体康复、认知节律调节和非药物干预,有新兴证据支持针对病毒后炎症和自主神经失调的药物治疗。长新冠在儿科人群中持续呈现动态的临床负担,近年来总体患病率有所下降,但在易感亚群中仍存在持续的发病率。整合的多学科随访模式和标准化的诊断标准对于优化儿科长新冠患者的长期临床管理和功能恢复至关重要。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
本研究旨在评估从早期大流行到当前时代,儿科患者长新冠的临床表现、演变及管理策略,因为该状况呈现出复杂的临床图景,并且随时间发生了变化。
设计与对象
系统综述了2020年至2026年间在PubMed、Scopus、Cochrane Library和Embase上发表的文献,重点关注0-18岁儿童和青少年的观察性研究、前瞻性队列分析以及评估感染后12周及以上急性后症状的临床试验数据。
核心方法
综述纳入了遵循PRISMA指南的研究,分析了儿科人群中感染后急性后症状(感染后12周及以上),重点在于临床轨迹、症状群及治疗管理策略。
主要结果
儿科长新冠的患病率从奥密克戎变种之前的10-25%下降到当前时代的2-8%,这主要受到新冠疫苗接种覆盖率和混合免疫的影响。最持久的临床表现从系统性疲劳、头痛和呼吸困难转变为持续性疲劳、神经认知功能障碍、情绪障碍和自主神经功能障碍。
解释与创新
作者将研究结果解释为突显了长新冠在儿童中的演变特性,随着时间推移症状学发生了显著变化,并强调了需要更新治疗策略的必要性。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现为制定整合的多学科随访模式和标准化的诊断标准提供了依据,以改善长新冠患儿的长期临床管理和功能恢复。
局限与证据强度
本研究的局限性在于依赖观察性数据以及疫情各阶段患病率的差异,但证据强度较高,得益于全面的系统综述方法和多样化的数据来源。}}.{

Long COVID in the pediatric population – clinical evolution, prevalence and management from the early pandemic to the current era: a systematic review

Authors: Paquita R. Q. Donoso, Jaime V. M. Muñoz, Joba E. I. Imbaquingo, Luis A. R. Vásconez, Eliana M. C. Peñafiel, Verónica J. M. Cuenca
Original abstract (English)
Post-acute sequelae of SARS-CoV-2 infection (PASC), or Long COVID, in the pediatric population present a complex clinical picture that has evolved from the initial pandemic waves to the current era characterized by omicron lineages and widespread vaccination. Understanding its prevalence, clinical trajectory, and evidence-based therapeutic strategies remains crucial for pediatric healthcare. Objective of the study was to systematically evaluate the clinical evolution, changing prevalence rates, predominant symptom clusters, and therapeutic management strategies of Long COVID in pediatric patients from the early pandemic to the current era. Systematic review of literature published between 2020 and 2026 across PubMed, Scopus, Cochrane Library, and Embase. Observational studies, prospective cohort analyses, and clinical trial data assessing post-acute symptoms (≥12 weeks post-infection) in children and adolescents aged 0–18 years were included following PRISMA guidelines. Reported prevalence of pediatric Long COVID varied significantly across pandemic phases, decreasing from 10–25% during pre-Omicron variants to 2–8% in the post-Omicron era, largely moderated by pediatric vaccination coverage and previous hybrid immunity. The most persistent clinical manifestations shifted from systemic fatigue, headache, and dyspnea to persistent fatigue, neurocognitive dysfunction ("brain fog"), mood disturbances, and dysautonomia (e.g., POTS-like symptoms). Management strategies remain primarily multidisciplinary and symptom-targeted, focusing on tailored physical rehabilitation, cognitive pacing, and non-pharmacological interventions, with emerging evidence supporting pharmacological targeting of post-viral inflammatory and autonomic dysregulation. Long COVID in pediatric populations continues to present a dynamic clinical burden, with reduced overall incidence in recent years but persistent morbidity in vulnerable subsets. Integrated, multidisciplinary follow-up models and standardized diagnostic criteria are essential to optimize long-term clinical management and functional recovery in pediatric patients.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to evaluate the clinical evolution, prevalence, and management strategies of Long COVID in pediatric patients from the early pandemic to the current era, as the condition presents a complex clinical picture that has changed over time.
Design and population
A systematic review of literature published between 2020 and 2026 was conducted across PubMed, Scopus, Cochrane Library, and Embase, focusing on observational studies, prospective cohort analyses, and clinical trial data assessing post-acute symptoms in children and adolescents aged 0–18 years.
Core methods
The review included studies following PRISMA guidelines, analyzing post-acute symptoms (≥12 weeks post-infection) in pediatric populations, with an emphasis on clinical trajectory, symptom clusters, and therapeutic management strategies.
Main results
The prevalence of pediatric Long COVID decreased from 10–25% during the pre-Omicron variants to 2–8% in the post-Omicron era, largely influenced by vaccination coverage and hybrid immunity. The most persistent clinical manifestations shifted from systemic fatigue, headache, and dyspnea to persistent fatigue, neurocognitive dysfunction, mood disturbances, and dysautonomia.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpreted the findings as highlighting the evolving nature of Long COVID in children, with a notable shift in symptomatology over time, and emphasized the need for updated therapeutic approaches.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings inform the development of integrated, multidisciplinary follow-up models and standardized diagnostic criteria to improve long-term clinical management and functional recovery in pediatric patients with Long COVID.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited by its reliance on observational data and the variability in prevalence rates across pandemic phases, but the evidence is strong due to the comprehensive systematic review methodology and inclusion of diverse data sources.

鼻内联合接种表达抗原不同的严重急性呼吸综合征冠状病毒2 S抗原的活疫苗疫苗可在仓鼠中诱导广泛且持久的免疫

Co-administration of intranasal parainfluenza virus vaccines expressing antigenically distinct SARS-CoV-2 S antigens elicits broad and durable immunity in hamsters
作者： Hong-Su Park, Yumiko Matsuoka, Celia P. Santos, Eleanor F. Duncan, Jaclyn A. Kaiser, Li-Juan Yang, Cindy Luongo, Xue-Qiao Liu, I‐Ting Teng, Peter D. Kwong
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
本研究旨在评估在仓鼠中,联合接种两种表达抗原不同的活化牛/人副流感病毒3型(B/HPIV3)媒介(分别表达祖先严重急性呼吸综合征冠状病毒2分离株和BA.5/Omicron变种的严重急性呼吸综合征冠状病毒2 刺突蛋白)所诱导的免疫广度和保护持久性。
设计与对象
本研究在仓鼠中进行,使用两种表达来自祖先严重急性呼吸综合征冠状病毒2分离株(B/HPIV3/S-6P)和BA.5/Omicron变种(B/HPIV3/S-BA.5-2P)的刺突蛋白的B/HPIV3 媒介。研究设计包括联合接种这些媒介以评估免疫广度和对BA.5/Omicron挑战的保护效果。
核心方法
研究人员开发了携带人类PIV3糖蛋白的活化B/HPIV3 媒介,并评估了其在仓鼠中诱导抗S IgG和IgA抗体水平的能力。他们还评估了中和抗体反应和对BA.5/Omicron挑战的保护效果。
主要结果
联合接种这两种B/HPIV3 媒介扩大了S特异性抗体反应的广度,覆盖了每种媒介单独接种所诱导的反应范围。接种混合疫苗的仓鼠对祖先和近期流行的严重急性呼吸综合征冠状病毒2株均产生了中和抗体,并在接种后5个月对BA.5/Omicron挑战表现出保护作用。
解释与创新
作者将这些发现解释为证据,表明联合接种表达抗原不同的严重急性呼吸综合征冠状病毒2 刺突蛋白的B/HPIV3 媒介可诱导广泛且持久的免疫,这可能为对抗新兴变种提供更好的保护。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现表明,这种黏膜免疫方法可能为严重急性呼吸综合征冠状病毒2提供持久且广泛的保护,从而可能改善针对新兴变种的疫苗策略。
局限与证据强度
本研究仅限于仓鼠模型,可能无法完全代表人类的免疫应答。然而,证据较强,因为它展示了对BA.5/Omicron挑战的持久保护,且未观察到体重减轻或炎症反应。

Co-administration of intranasal parainfluenza virus vaccines expressing antigenically distinct SARS-CoV-2 S antigens elicits broad and durable immunity in hamsters

Authors: Hong-Su Park, Yumiko Matsuoka, Celia P. Santos, Eleanor F. Duncan, Jaclyn A. Kaiser, Li-Juan Yang, Cindy Luongo, Xue-Qiao Liu, I‐Ting Teng, Peter D. Kwong
Original abstract (English)
ABSTRACT Intranasal COVID-19 vaccines with the ability to induce broad and durable mucosal and systemic immunity would be useful as stand-alone vaccines or in combination with injectable vaccines. We evaluated in hamsters the breadth of immunity and durability of protection elicited by co-administration of two live-attenuated bovine/human parainfluenza virus type 3 (B/HPIV3) vectors expressing antigenically distinct prefusion-stabilized S proteins of the ancestral SARS-CoV-2 isolate (B/HPIV3/S-6P) or the BA.5/Omicron variant (B/HPIV3/S-BA.5-2P). These vectors are being developed as bivalent pediatric vaccines against HPIV3 and SARS-CoV-2 and are based on bovine PIV3 with the fusion (F) and hemagglutinin-neuraminidase (HN) glycoproteins replaced by those of human PIV3. To broaden the S-specific antibody response, we evaluated co-administration of these B/HPIV3 S-expressing vectors. Each B/HPIV3 S-expressing vector induced robust serum anti-S IgG and IgA antibody levels to the antigen-matched S protein that were sustained for at least 5 months. Co-administration increased the breadth of the S-specific antibody response, spanning the breadth of the response elicited by each vector individually. Hamsters immunized intranasally with the mixture of vectors developed neutralizing antibodies to ancestral and recently circulating SARS-CoV-2 strains and were protected against BA.5/Omicron challenge 5 months after immunization, with no weight loss, SARS-CoV-2 challenge virus replication, or increase in host inflammatory cytokines in the airways after the challenge, indicating durable protection. Thus, intranasal co-administration of live-attenuated B/HPIV3 expressing antigenically distinct S proteins induced broad and durable antibody responses, and long-term protection against BA.5/Omicron challenge. This approach warrants further development and may better protect against emerging SARS-CoV-2 variants. IMPORTANCE Intranasal SARS-CoV-2 vaccines that induce systemic and local mucosal immunity hold promise, especially as booster vaccines against SARS-CoV-2 variants. We developed live-attenuated bovine/human parainfluenza virus type 3 (B/HPIV3)-vectored vaccines as bivalent pediatric vaccines against PIV3 and SARS-CoV-2. Co-administration in a single dose of two B/HPIV3 vectors, one expressing the spike protein S from the ancestral Wuhan-Hu-1 strain and one expressing S of BA.5/Omicron, induced broad serum anti-S antibody responses that remained strong for at least 5 months. Hamsters challenged with the BA.5/Omicron strain 5 months after immunization were protected from weight loss, inflammatory responses, and challenge virus replication in the airways. Thus, in the hamster model, intranasal co-administration of live-attenuated B/HPIV3 expressing SARS-CoV-2 S elicits protection durable over several months, and combining two antigenically distinct B/HPIV3 S-expressing vectors substantially broadens the antibody response. This mucosal immunization approach may provide durable and broad protection against SARS-CoV-2.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to evaluate the breadth of immunity and durability of protection elicited by co-administration of two live-attenuated bovine/human parainfluenza virus type 3 (B/HPIV3) vectors expressing antigenically distinct SARS-CoV-2 spike proteins in hamsters.
Design and population
The study was conducted in hamsters using two B/HPIV3 vectors expressing the spike protein from the ancestral SARS-CoV-2 isolate (B/HPIV3/S-6P) and the BA.5/Omicron variant (B/HPIV3/S-BA.5-2P). The design involved co-administration of these vectors to assess immune responses and protection against BA.5/Omicron challenge.
Core methods
The researchers developed live-attenuated B/HPIV3 vectors with human PIV3 glycoproteins and evaluated their ability to induce anti-S IgG and IgA antibody levels in hamsters. They also assessed neutralizing antibody responses and protection against BA.5/Omicron challenge.
Main results
Co-administration of the two B/HPIV3 vectors increased the breadth of the S-specific antibody response, spanning the breadth elicited by each vector individually. Hamsters immunized with the mixture developed neutralizing antibodies to both ancestral and recently circulating SARS-CoV-2 strains and were protected against BA.5/Omicron challenge 5 months post-immunization.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpreted these findings as evidence that co-administration of B/HPIV3 vectors expressing antigenically distinct SARS-CoV-2 spike proteins can elicit broad and durable immunity, which may offer better protection against emerging variants.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings suggest that this mucosal immunization approach could provide durable and broad protection against SARS-CoV-2, potentially improving vaccine strategies for combating emerging variants.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited to a hamster model, which may not fully represent human immune responses. However, the evidence is strong as it demonstrates durable protection against BA.5/Omicron challenge with no weight loss or inflammatory responses observed.

Comparative Burden of COVID-19 in Adults with Rheumatic Diseases and Immunocompetent Individuals: A Population-Based Cohort Study in Quebec, Canada, 2020-2023

Comparative Burden of COVID-19 in Adults with Rheumatic Diseases and Immunocompetent Individuals: A Population-Based Cohort Study in Quebec, Canada, 2020-2023
作者： Sara Carazo, Inés Colmegna, Sasha Bernatsky, Paul R. Fortin, Sonia Jean, Denis Charles Talbot, Louis Rochette, Charles-Antoine Guay, Michael Libman, Michaël Desjardins
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
The study aimed to compare the burden of severe COVID-19 between individuals with systemic autoimmune rheumatic diseases (SARDs) and immunocompetent individuals, addressing the knowledge gap about the impact of rheumatic diseases on pandemic outcomes.
设计与对象
本基于人群的队列研究研究分析了加拿大魁北克地区2020年至2023年的行政数据集,包括81,434名SARDs患者、2,020,356名有合并症的个体以及3,144,700名无合并症的个体。
核心方法
The study used administrative datasets to estimate crude and age- and sex-standardized incidences of SARS-CoV-2 and severe COVID-19, applying Cox models to derive adjusted hazard ratios (aHRs) for infection, hospitalization, and ICU/death across different pandemic periods.
主要结果
The SARDS cohort had higher risks of hospitalization and ICU/death compared to immunocompetent individuals during the pre-vaccination period (aHRs: 3.4, 95%CI:2.8–4.1; and 4.0, 95%CI:2.8–5.7, respectively), with these risks persisting through the Omicron period (aHRs: 4.5, 95%CI:4.0–4.9; and 4.4, 95%CI:3.6–5.4, respectively).
解释与创新
作者认为,SARDs患者,特别是那些接受高度免疫抑制治疗的患者,面临更高的严重结果风险,强调了需要采取针对性的公共卫生策略。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现为在未来的疫情中优先考虑SARDs患者提供了依据,强调了在公共卫生措施中制定针对性监测和干预措施的重要性。
局限与证据强度
本研究依赖于行政数据,可能会引入偏倚,且由于研究设计的观察性质和潜在的混杂因素,证据强度中等。

Comparative Burden of COVID-19 in Adults with Rheumatic Diseases and Immunocompetent Individuals: A Population-Based Cohort Study in Quebec, Canada, 2020-2023

Authors: Sara Carazo, Inés Colmegna, Sasha Bernatsky, Paul R. Fortin, Sonia Jean, Denis Charles Talbot, Louis Rochette, Charles-Antoine Guay, Michael Libman, Michaël Desjardins
Original abstract (English)
Abstract Introduction Across major phases of the COVID-19 pandemic (March 2020-April 2023), we compared the burden of severe COVID-19 among individuals with systemic autoimmune rheumatic diseases (SARDs) versus immunocompetent individuals with and without comorbidities. Methods This population-based cohort study used administrative datasets to estimate crude and age-and sex-standardized SARS-CoV-2 and severe COVID-19 incidences among adults > 18 years with SARDs, chronic comorbidities, or no comorbidities. Relative to the latter, we used Cox models to derive adjusted hazard ratios (aHRs) for infection, hospitalization, and intensive care unit admission or death (ICU/death) during the pre-vaccination (March–December 2020), vaccination pre-Omicron (December 2020– December 2021), and Omicron (December 2021–April 2023) periods. Results We included 81,434 adults with SARDs, and 2,020,356 with comorbidities versus 3,144,700 without comorbidities. Despite similar adjusted infection rates, the SARDS cohort had higher risks of hospitalization and ICU/death during the pre-vaccination period (aHRs: 3.4, 95%CI:2.8–4.1; and 4.0, 95%CI:2.8–5.7, respectively), persisting through the Omicron period (aHRs: 4.5, 95%CI:4.0–4.9; and 4.4, 95%CI:3.6–5.4, respectively). Among SARDS, rheumatoid arthritis, psoriatic arthritis and systemic lupus erythematosus contributed highest burden, with comparable hospitalization and mortality rates among younger rheumatoid arthritis versus healthy older adults. Highly immunosuppressive rituximab-or mycophenolate-treated patients had highest hospitalization and ICU/death risks, particularly during the Omicron period (aHRs: 11.9, 95%CI:9.9–14.3; and 17.6, 95%CI:13.0– 23.9, respectively). Conclusion Individuals with SARDs, especially receiving highly immunosuppressive therapies, experienced elevated COVID-19 burden and severe outcome risks throughout the pandemic, warranting their prioritized consideration during future public health emergencies.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to compare the burden of severe COVID-19 between individuals with systemic autoimmune rheumatic diseases (SARDs) and immunocompetent individuals, addressing the knowledge gap about the impact of rheumatic diseases on pandemic outcomes.
Design and population
This population-based cohort study analyzed administrative datasets from Quebec, Canada, spanning 2020 to 2023, including 81,434 adults with SARDs, 2,020,356 with comorbidities, and 3,144,700 without comorbidities.
Core methods
The study used administrative datasets to estimate crude and age- and sex-standardized incidences of SARS-CoV-2 and severe COVID-19, applying Cox models to derive adjusted hazard ratios (aHRs) for infection, hospitalization, and ICU/death across different pandemic periods.
Main results
The SARDS cohort had higher risks of hospitalization and ICU/death compared to immunocompetent individuals during the pre-vaccination period (aHRs: 3.4, 95%CI:2.8–4.1; and 4.0, 95%CI:2.8–5.7, respectively), with these risks persisting through the Omicron period (aHRs: 4.5, 95%CI:4.0–4.9; and 4.4, 95%CI:3.6–5.4, respectively).
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpreted that individuals with SARDs, particularly those on highly immunosuppressive therapies, faced elevated risks of severe outcomes, highlighting the need for targeted public health strategies.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings inform the prioritization of SARDS patients in public health measures, emphasizing the importance of tailored surveillance and interventions during future pandemics.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study relies on administrative data, which may introduce biases, and the evidence is moderate due to the observational nature of the design and potential confounding factors.

针对严重急性呼吸综合征冠状病毒2保守主蛋白酶的变体特异性G-四链体配体的计算机模拟研究

In Silico Investigation of Variant-Specific G-Quadruplex Ligands Targeting the Conserved Main Protease of SARS-CoV-2
作者： N. Prompat, V. Tipmanee, P. Nadee, A. Phongdara, A. Nualla-ong
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
当前严重急性呼吸综合征冠状病毒2大流行凸显了开发对新兴变体有效的抗病毒药物的必要性。本研究旨在探索G-四链体(G4)配体作为针对保守主蛋白酶(Mpro)的变体特异性抑制剂的潜力。
设计与对象
这项体外研究分析了5.8百万条GISAID序列,以识别突变在Mpro酶中的存在。它重点研究了严重急性呼吸综合征冠状病毒2变体,包括奥密克戎(JN.1),并选择了具有代表性的Mpro结构进行筛选。
核心方法
本研究采用分子对接、分子动力学模拟和MM/GBSA计算来评估配体与Mpro酶的相互作用。同时进行了ADMET预测,以评估药物样特性及潜在风险。
主要结果
对GISAID序列的分析显示,突变 K90R(Alpha)、G15S(Lambda)和P132H(Omicron,JN.1)等突变频繁出现。确定了四个配体G4L3501、G4L3503、G4L3505和G4L3506,它们通过与His41、Cys145、Glu166和Gln189等保守残基的稳定相互作用占据催化口袋。
解释与创新
按残基分解能量分析证实了活性位点残基对配体的强稳定作用,与奈玛特韦(PF-07321332)的结合模式一致。这表明G4配体可能成为跨变体Mpro抑制的有前景的骨架。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现支持开发G4配体作为潜在的抗病毒药物用于严重急性呼吸综合征冠状病毒2,并强调将基因组监测与分子建模相结合在指导药物发现中的价值。
局限与证据强度
该研究仅限于计算机分析,需要进行实验测定来确认抑制活性并完善药代动力学特性。由于依赖计算方法,证据强度中等。

In Silico Investigation of Variant-Specific G-Quadruplex Ligands Targeting the Conserved Main Protease of SARS-CoV-2

Authors: N. Prompat, V. Tipmanee, P. Nadee, A. Phongdara, A. Nualla-ong
Original abstract (English)
Abstract The ongoing SARS-CoV-2 pandemic highlights the need for antivirals effective across emerging variants. Targeting the conserved main protease (Mpro), a key enzyme in viral replication, this study explored G-quadruplex (G4) ligands as variant-specific inhibitors. Analysis of 5.8 million GISAID sequences revealed frequent but catalytically distal mutations K90R (Alpha), G15S (Lambda), and P132H (Omicron, JN.1) guiding the selection of representative Mpro structures for in silico screening. Molecular docking, molecular dynamics simulations, and MM/GBSA calculations identified four ligands G4L3501 (–36.63 kcal/mol), G4L3503 (–40.39 kcal/mol), G4L3505 (–40.54 kcal/mol), and G4L3506 (–20.32 kcal/mol) that stably occupied the catalytic pocket through conserved interactions with His41, Cys145, Glu166, and Gln189. Per-residue energy decomposition confirmed strong stabilization by active-site residues, consistent with the nirmatrelvir (PF-07321332) binding mode. ADMET predictions suggested moderate permeability and acceptable drug-likeness but limited intestinal absorption and potential CYP inhibition liabilities. Among candidates, G4L3505 and G4L3506 showed the most favourable overall profiles. These findings support G4 ligands as promising scaffolds for cross-variant SARS-CoV-2 Mpro inhibition and highlight the value of integrating genomic surveillance with molecular modeling. Experimental assays are required to confirm their inhibitory activity and refine pharmacokinetic properties.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The ongoing SARS-CoV-2 pandemic highlights the need for antivirals effective across emerging variants. This study aimed to explore G-quadruplex (G4) ligands as variant-specific inhibitors targeting the conserved main protease (Mpro).
Design and population
This in silico study analyzed 5.8 million GISAID sequences to identify mutations in the Mpro enzyme. It focused on SARS-CoV-2 variants, including Omicron (JN.1), and selected representative Mpro structures for screening.
Core methods
The study used molecular docking, molecular dynamics simulations, and MM/GBSA calculations to evaluate ligand interactions with the Mpro enzyme. ADMET predictions were also conducted to assess drug-like properties and potential liabilities.
Main results
Analysis of GISAID sequences revealed frequent mutations K90R (Alpha), G15S (Lambda), and P132H (Omicron, JN.1). Four ligands, G4L3501, G4L3503, G4L3505, and G4L3506, were identified as stably occupying the catalytic pocket through conserved interactions with His41, Cys145, Glu166, and Gln189.
Interpretation and novelty
Per-residue energy decomposition confirmed strong stabilization by active-site residues, consistent with the nirmatrelvir (PF-07321332) binding mode. This suggests that G4 ligands could serve as promising scaffolds for cross-variant Mpro inhibition.
Scientific and public-health significance
These findings support the development of G4 ligands as potential antivirals for SARS-CoV-2 and highlight the value of integrating genomic surveillance with molecular modeling to guide drug discovery.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited to in silico analysis, and experimental assays are required to confirm inhibitory activity and refine pharmacokinetic properties. The evidence strength is moderate due to the reliance on computational methods.

严重急性呼吸综合征冠状病毒2 严重程度和 疫苗接种、肺炎和中风风险:一项全国性巢式病例对照研究

SARS-CoV-2 severity and vaccination, pneumonia, and the risk of Stroke: A nationwide, nested case-control study
作者： Nika Zur, Nili Stien, Walid Saliba, Galit Weinstein
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
Infections, including SARS-CoV-2, may increase stroke risk, but the roles of COVID-19 severity, vaccination, and comparison with other respiratory infections remain unclear.
设计与对象
This study examined the association between COVID-19, its severity, and vaccination status with the incidence of new-onset total stroke, intracerebral hemorrhage (ICH) and ischemic stroke in a nationwide sample of adults aged ≥50 years.
核心方法
以色列最大的医疗保健提供者进行了一项巢式病例对照研究。从 2020 年 3 月至 2022 年 5 月对无中风的个体进行了随访。事件总数、ICH 和缺血性中风病例按年龄、性别和索引日期与对照按 1:10 进行匹配。
主要结果
Among 1,198,088 eligible participants, 11,974 individuals with incident total stroke were matched to 119,740 controls. COVID-19 was associated with an increased stroke risk (OR=1.23; 95% CI 1.16-1.30; P < 0.001). Vaccination was associated with lower stroke risk, although this observational association warrants cautious interpretation.
解释与创新
The authors interpreted that COVID-19 requiring hospitalization confers an acute, time-limited increase in stroke risk, of a magnitude somewhat lower than that observed after pneumonia.
科研与公卫意义
这些发现支持因呼吸道感染住院后针对脑血管的 监测 治疗。
局限与证据强度
该研究是观察性的,因此 疫苗接种 与较低中风风险之间的关联需要谨慎解释。

SARS-CoV-2 severity and vaccination, pneumonia, and the risk of Stroke: A nationwide, nested case-control study

Authors: Nika Zur, Nili Stien, Walid Saliba, Galit Weinstein
Original abstract (English)
Abstract Background : Infections, including SARS-CoV-2, may increase stroke risk, but the roles of COVID-19 severity, vaccination, and comparison with other respiratory infections remain unclear. This study examined the association between COVID-19, its severity, and vaccination status with the incidence of new-onset total stroke, intracerebral hemorrhage (ICH) and ischemic stroke in a nationwide sample of adults aged ≥50 years. Methods: A nested case-control study was conducted within the largest healthcare provider in Israel. Stroke-free individuals were followed-up from March 2020 to May 2022. Incident total, ICH and ischemic stroke cases were matched 1:10 to controls by age, sex, and index date. Laboratory-confirmed SARS-CoV-2 infection, COVID-19 severity, vaccination status, and pneumonia were ascertained from electronic health records, and multivariable conditional logistic regression estimated odds ratios (ORs) for stroke. Results: Among 1,198,088 eligible participants, 11,974 individuals with incident total stroke were matched to 119,740 controls. COVID-19 was associated with an increased stroke risk (OR=1.23; 95% CI 1.16-1.30; P Conclusions: COVID-19 requiring hospitalization confers an acute, time-limited increase in stroke risk, of a magnitude somewhat lower than that observed after pneumonia. These findings support targeted cerebrovascular surveillance following hospitalization for respiratory infection. Vaccination was associated with lower stroke risk, although this observational association warrants cautious interpretation.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Infections, including SARS-CoV-2, may increase stroke risk, but the roles of COVID-19 severity, vaccination, and comparison with other respiratory infections remain unclear.
Design and population
This study examined the association between COVID-19, its severity, and vaccination status with the incidence of new-onset total stroke, intracerebral hemorrhage (ICH) and ischemic stroke in a nationwide sample of adults aged ≥50 years.
Core methods
A nested case-control study was conducted within the largest healthcare provider in Israel. Stroke-free individuals were followed-up from March 2020 to May 2022. Incident total, ICH and ischemic stroke cases were matched 1:10 to controls by age, sex, and index date.
Main results
Among 1,198,088 eligible participants, 11,974 individuals with incident total stroke were matched to 119,740 controls. COVID-19 was associated with an increased stroke risk (OR=1.23; 95% CI 1.16-1.30; P < 0.001). Vaccination was associated with lower stroke risk, although this observational association warrants cautious interpretation.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpreted that COVID-19 requiring hospitalization confers an acute, time-limited increase in stroke risk, of a magnitude somewhat lower than that observed after pneumonia.
Scientific and public-health significance
These findings support targeted cerebrovascular surveillance following hospitalization for respiratory infection.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is observational and thus the association between vaccination and lower stroke risk warrants cautious interpretation.

Immunogenicity and vaccine effectiveness of COVID-19 vaccines in people on immunosuppressive therapies

Immunogenicity and vaccine effectiveness of COVID-19 vaccines in people on immunosuppressive therapies
作者： Eva Stadler, Shanchita R. Khan, Karen M Elias, Ece Egilmezer, Chansavath Phetsouphanh, Priyanka Hastak, Rehana Hewavisenti, Ruchika Vinod Joshi, Tari Turner, Deborah Cromer
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在调查接受免疫抑制治疗的人群中中和抗体与 严重急性呼吸综合征冠状病毒2 保护之间的关系,因为保护相关数据通常排除这些个体。
设计与对象
进行了一项系统性搜索,以确定在接受免疫抑制治疗的血液恶性肿瘤、神经系统疾病、风湿性疾病和混合免疫介导的炎症性疾病患者以及健康对照中,mRNA 疫苗接种 后 严重急性呼吸综合征冠状病毒2 结合抗体水平的研究。
核心方法
该研究使用了之前对 疫苗 对具有相同基础条件的人的有效性进行分析的数据,并将这些估计值与结合抗体水平荟萃分析得出的预测抗体水平联系起来。
主要结果
接受不同免疫抑制 队列研究 的 队列研究 的结合抗体水平各不相同,在接受 B 细胞耗竭和 S1P 抑制剂治疗的患者中观察到结合抗体水平最低。预测的中和抗体水平与 疫苗 对感染和住院的有效性相关。
解释与创新
作者将研究结果解释为表明抗体水平与 疫苗 的有效性相关,但强调与健康人群相比,免疫抑制个体在任​​何给定抗体水平下的保护作用较低。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现可以为监测免疫抑制个体的 疫苗 反应的策略提供信息,并指导为该弱势群体开发更有效的 疫苗 方案。
局限与证据强度
该研究依赖于先前分析的数据和预测的抗体水平,这可能会限制抗体水平和保护之间相关性的准确性,从而导致中等置信度评估。

Immunogenicity and vaccine effectiveness of COVID-19 vaccines in people on immunosuppressive therapies

Authors: Eva Stadler, Shanchita R. Khan, Karen M Elias, Ece Egilmezer, Chansavath Phetsouphanh, Priyanka Hastak, Rehana Hewavisenti, Ruchika Vinod Joshi, Tari Turner, Deborah Cromer
Original abstract (English)
Abstract Neutralizing antibodies are a correlate of protection (CoP) of SARS-CoV-2 vaccine efficacy. However, data informing CoPs often explicitly exclude people on immunosuppressive therapies who are at an increased risk of symptomatic and severe COVID-19, delayed viral clearance, and death. Investigating the relationship between antibodies and protection for people on immunosuppressive therapies could provide insights into mechanisms of protection. We performed a systematic search to identify studies reporting SARS-CoV-2 binding antibody levels after mRNA vaccination in people with hematological malignancies, neurological disorders, rheumatic diseases, and mixed immune-mediated inflammatory disorders (IMIDs) on immunosuppressive therapies including B-cell depletion, JAK inhibitors, and S1P inhibitors, as well as healthy controls. Binding antibody levels varied between cohorts receiving different immunosuppressive treatments. Compared to healthy controls the lowest antibody levels were observed in subjects treated with B-cell depletion (54.5-fold reduction, 95% CI: 34.5-86.2) and S1P inhibitors (24.8-fold reduction, 95% CI: 13.2-46.7). To assess the association between antibody level and protection, we used data from a previous study of COVID-19 vaccine effectiveness in people with the same underlying conditions. We linked vaccine effectiveness estimates with the predicted antibody level in people on immunosuppressive therapies (using antibody data including this meta-analysis of binding antibody levels). Predicted neutralizing antibody levels were correlated with vaccine effectiveness both for infection and hospitalization (p<0.0001 and p<0.0001, respectively). However, we found that for any given antibody level, people who are immunosuppressed had lower protection against infection and hospitalization than a healthy population.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to investigate the relationship between neutralizing antibodies and protection against SARS-CoV-2 in people on immunosuppressive therapies, as data on correlates of protection often exclude these individuals.
Design and population
A systematic search was conducted to identify studies on SARS-CoV-2 binding antibody levels after mRNA vaccination in people with hematological malignancies, neurological disorders, rheumatic diseases, and mixed immune-mediated inflammatory disorders on immunosuppressive therapies, as well as healthy controls.
Core methods
The study used data from a previous analysis of vaccine effectiveness in people with the same underlying conditions and linked these estimates with predicted antibody levels derived from a meta-analysis of binding antibody levels.
Main results
Binding antibody levels varied across cohorts receiving different immunosuppressive treatments, with the lowest levels observed in those treated with B-cell depletion and S1P inhibitors. Predicted neutralizing antibody levels were correlated with vaccine effectiveness for both infection and hospitalization.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpreted the findings as indicating that antibody levels are associated with vaccine effectiveness, but emphasized that immunosuppressed individuals have lower protection at any given antibody level compared to healthy populations.
Scientific and public-health significance
These findings could inform strategies for monitoring vaccine responses in immunosuppressed individuals and guide the development of more effective vaccination protocols for this vulnerable group.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study relies on data from a previous analysis and predicted antibody levels, which may introduce limitations in the accuracy of the correlation between antibody levels and protection, resulting in a moderate confidence assessment.

Immune dysfunction in chronic lymphocytic leukemia : translational and epidemiological studies during the COVID-19 pandemic

Immune dysfunction in chronic lymphocytic leukemia : translational and epidemiological studies during the COVID-19 pandemic
作者： Lisa Blixt
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
This study aimed to investigate the impact of SARS-CoV-2 infection and vaccination on individuals with chronic lymphocytic leukemia (CLL) during the COVID-19 pandemic, given the known immune deficiencies in CLL patients.
设计与对象
该研究包括五项不同的调查。研究I检查了大流行早期斯德哥尔摩地区的CLL患者,研究II在COVAXID试验中比较了CLL患者与健康对照,研究III侧重于康复患者的混合免疫,研究IV使用国家登记处分析了瑞典疫苗的吸收率,研究V评估了大流行前三年的死亡率和住院率。
核心方法
研究 I 从病历中收集临床数据,并通过重复的血液和唾液样本对患者进行长达 12 个月的跟踪。研究 II 使用了 COVAXID 试验的数据,包括 疫苗接种 前后的血液和唾液样本。研究 IV 利用六个国家健康和社会人口统计登记处来跟踪 疫苗接种 随着时间的推移的吸收情况。
主要结果
在研究 I 中,77% 的 CLL 患者需要住院治疗,24% 需要重症监护,大约 30% 死亡。在研究 II 中,60% 的 CLL 患者出现血清转化,抗体滴度较低,而 29% 的患者观察到 T 细胞反应。在研究 IV 中,94% 的 CLL 患者接受了两剂 疫苗,88% 接受了三剂,78% 接受了四剂,56% 接受了五剂 疫苗。在研究 V 中,与对照组相比,CLL 患者 90 天全因 死亡率 的调整后风险比为 1.70,年轻患者和某些 治疗 患者的风险较高。
解释与创新
研究结果表明,CLL 患者因 严重急性呼吸综合征冠状病毒2 感染而出现更严重的结果,但 疫苗 剂量似乎可以降低不良结果的风险。该研究还强调了 CLL 人群中不同人群对 疫苗 的吸收差异。
科研与公卫意义
结果为 CLL 患者的 疫苗接种 和感染预防策略提供了信息,强调需要量身定制的方法并解决 疫苗 获取和摄取方面的差异。
局限与证据强度
该研究依赖于登记数据和医疗记录,这可能会引入偏差或缺失信息。然而,由于使用了多个国家数据集和对 疫苗 吸收率的纵向跟踪,证据是强有力的。

Immune dysfunction in chronic lymphocytic leukemia : translational and epidemiological studies during the COVID-19 pandemic

Authors: Lisa Blixt
Original abstract (English)
Chronic lymphocytic leukemia (CLL) is a chronic malignancy of mature B lymphocytes with increasing prevalence in Sweden. CLL is associated with substantial immune deficiency, making patients more susceptible to infections and less responsive to conventional vaccines. CLL therapy may further aggravate immunosuppression. The COVID-19 pandemic therefore raised important questions about the impact of SARS-CoV-2 infection and vaccination in individuals with CLL. This thesis addresses some of these questions. Study I investigated the clinical and immunological outcome of SARS-CoV-2 infection in CLL patients at the beginning of the pandemic. We included all known and PCR-verified COVID-19 cases among CLL patients in the Stockholm region between 1 March 2020 and 31 March 2021. Clinical data were collected from medical records, and patients who recovered were followed with repeated blood and saliva samples for up to 12 months to assess B and T cell responses. Among 60 patients, 77% required hospitalization, 24% intensive care, and approximately 30% died. Among convalescents (n=40), 82% seroconverted, 68% had detectable SARS-CoV-2-specific antibodies in saliva, and T cell immune responses was detected in 14/17 (82%). Serum antibodies remained detectable in approximately 75% at 6 and 12 months. Clinical outcomes improved during the second half of the study period. Study II evaluated B and T cell responses after two doses of the Pfizer-BioNTech mRNA vaccine BNT162b2 in 52 CLL patients and 41 healthy controls participating in the COVAXID prospective trial. The CLL cohort included patients with untreated indolent CLL (n=14), previous immunochemotherapy including rituximab >12 months earlier (n=14), ongoing BTKi treatment (n=14), or discontinued BTKi treatment due to remission (n=10). Sixty percent of CLL patients seroconverted, with low antibody titers (median 25 U/ml), compared to 100% of controls (median 2600 U/ml). T cell responses occurred in 29% of CLL patients versus 60% of controls. Responses were more frequent after discontinuation of BTKi, whereas patients currently receiving BTKi treatment had the lowest seroconversion and T cell response rates. Fifteen patients (29%) were double-negative, and 11/15 had ongoing BTKi treatment. Study III investigated hybrid immunity in the cohort from Study I, i.e. patients who had recovered from COVID-19 and subsequently received SARS-CoV-2 vaccination. Blood and saliva samples were collected before vaccination, after the second vaccine dose, and before and after the third dose. Serum antibody titers increased significantly after two doses, from a median of 47 U/ml to 12,500 U/ml. Titers decreased by approximately 50% between the second and third dose (median interval 5 months), to then increase even further after the third dose. SARS-CoV-2-specific T cell reactivity and salivary antibodies also increased after vaccination, but mainly after the third dose. Study IV examined SARS-CoV-2 vaccine uptake among individuals with CLL in Sweden using six national health and sociodemographic registries. We included 6,304 individuals aged 18-90 years diagnosed with CLL up to November 30, 2022, and followed vaccination uptake from December 27, 2020 (date of first SARS-CoV-2 vaccination in Sweden), to February 28, 2023. Overall uptake was higher than age-standardized nationwide uptake: 94% received two doses, 88% three doses, 78% four doses, and 56% five doses. However, 253 individuals remained unvaccinated, and substantial disparities were observed by age, region of birth, and income. These disparities were greatest for the fourth and fifth doses; among individuals born abroad and in the lowest income quartile, uptake was only 49% and 24%, respectively, for the fourth and fifth doses. Study V analyzed COVID-19-related mortality and hospitalization over the first three years of the pandemic in Sweden. Cross-linked data from ten national registries were used to compare individuals with CLL with the general population, stratified by age, time period/SARS-CoV-2 variant, and vaccination status. Among 8,275,839 individuals born 1930-2003, including 6,653 with CLL, 2,088,163 first SARS-CoV-2 infections were identified between February 1, 2020, and February 27, 2023, including 1,289 in individuals with CLL. The adjusted risk ratio (aRR) for 90-day all-cause mortality was 1.70 in CLL versus comparators and 4.17 among those younger than 65 years; the overall mortality rate in this age group was 3.6%. Overall mortality was highest during the Wild-type period but remained 12% during the Omicron era, with an aRR of 1.49. CLL patients receiving Bcl-2i, BTKi, PI3Ki, or corticosteroids at time of infection showed increased risk of hospitalization and death compared to CLL patients not treated with these drugs. However, aRRs for poor outcomes decreased with each vaccine dose vs comparators with the corresponding vaccination status, suggesting protection against severe COVID-19 also in individuals with CLL. Question and background
This study aimed to investigate the impact of SARS-CoV-2 infection and vaccination on individuals with chronic lymphocytic leukemia (CLL) during the COVID-19 pandemic, given the known immune deficiencies in CLL patients.
Design and population
The study included five distinct investigations. Study I examined CLL patients in the Stockholm region during the early pandemic, Study II compared CLL patients with healthy controls in the COVAXID trial, Study III focused on hybrid immunity in recovered patients, Study IV analyzed vaccine uptake in Sweden using national registries, and Study V evaluated mortality and hospitalization rates across the first three years of the pandemic.
Core methods
Study I collected clinical data from medical records and followed patients with repeated blood and saliva samples for up to 12 months. Study II used data from the COVAXID trial, including blood and saliva samples before and after vaccination. Study IV utilized six national health and sociodemographic registries to track vaccination uptake over time.
Main results
In Study I, 77% of CLL patients required hospitalization, 24% needed intensive care, and approximately 30% died. In Study II, 60% of CLL patients seroconverted with low antibody titers, while T cell responses were observed in 29% of patients. In Study IV, 94% of CLL patients received two doses, 88% three doses, 78% four doses, and 56% five doses of the vaccine. In Study V, the adjusted risk ratio for 90-day all-cause mortality was 1.70 in CLL patients compared to comparators, with higher risks among younger patients and those on certain treatments.
Interpretation and novelty
The findings suggest that CLL patients experience more severe outcomes from SARS-CoV-2 infection, but vaccine doses appear to reduce the risk of poor outcomes. The study also highlights disparities in vaccine uptake among different demographic groups within the CLL population.
Scientific and public-health significance
The results inform strategies for vaccination and infection prevention in CLL patients, emphasizing the need for tailored approaches and addressing disparities in vaccine access and uptake.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study relies on registry data and medical records, which may introduce bias or missing information. However, the evidence is robust due to the use of multiple national datasets and longitudinal tracking of vaccine uptake.

Increased In-Hospital Mortality in Immunocompromised Individuals Hospitalized With COVID-19 During the Global Pandemic: A Multinational Cohort Study in the EuCARE Project.

Increased In-Hospital Mortality in Immunocompromised Individuals Hospitalized With COVID-19 During the Global Pandemic: A Multinational Cohort Study in the EuCARE Project.
作者： Ashley O Roen, Pontus Hedberg, Joana P Ventura Pereira, Maurizio Zazzi, Dovile Juozapaite, Milosz Parczewski, João Domingos, Francis Drobniewski, Giulia Marchetti, Luca Carioti
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
The study aimed to investigate whether immunocompromising conditions (ICCs) still pose an increased mortality risk during the Omicron era of the COVID-19 pandemic. Although coronavirus disease 2019 (COVID-19) is no longer a public health emergency, it remains the most prevalent circulating infectious-like illness in Europe.
设计与对象
A multinational cohort study was conducted across EuCARE sites in 8 countries among adults hospitalized with COVID-19 between 2020 and 2023. A total of 42,488 individuals were included, of whom 1,675 (3.9%) had an ICC.
核心方法
ICCs and COVID-19 pneumonia at hospitalization were defined using clinical information and ICD-10 codes. Logistic regression and counterfactual mediation analysis were used to compare 28-day in-hospital mortality risk associated with ICCs, using COVID-19 pneumonia and vaccination at hospital entry as intermediates. Proportion of the total effect of ICCs mediated and the controlled direct effects (CDEs) were calculated. The study also formally tested for interaction between SARS-CoV-2 variants and ICCs for mortality risk.
主要结果
ICCs were associated with increased mortality (OR, 1.49 [95% CI, 1.25-1.79]). There was no evidence for an attenuation during the Omicron phase (P value for interaction = .60). Mediation analyses showed that the total effect of ICCs was mediated by vaccination but only weakly by pneumonia. With Omicron, the excess mortality associated with ICC was higher under the scenario that everyone in the cohort was to develop COVID-19 pneumonia (CDE, 1.22 [95% CI, .09-1.65]).
解释与创新
作者得出的结论是,即使在 Omicron 时代,ICC 仍然是院内死亡的一个重要危险因素,特别是如果感染导致肺炎的发展。这一发现凸显了尽管出现了 Omicron 变体,但免疫功能低下的情况对 死亡率 结果的持续影响。
科研与公卫意义
The findings suggest that immunocompromised individuals hospitalized with COVID-19 continue to face heightened mortality risks, even with the Omicron variant. This has important implications for targeted surveillance, diagnosis, and management strategies for immunocompromised patients during future outbreaks.
局限与证据强度
该研究的局限性在于其依赖临床信息和 ICD-10 代码来定义 ICC 和肺炎,这可能会引入偏差。证据是中等的,因为该研究基于仅摘要分析,没有提供详细的统计或方法学信息。

Increased In-Hospital Mortality in Immunocompromised Individuals Hospitalized With COVID-19 During the Global Pandemic: A Multinational Cohort Study in the EuCARE Project.

Authors: Ashley O Roen, Pontus Hedberg, Joana P Ventura Pereira, Maurizio Zazzi, Dovile Juozapaite, Milosz Parczewski, João Domingos, Francis Drobniewski, Giulia Marchetti, Luca Carioti
Original abstract (English)
BACKGROUND: Although coronavirus disease 2019 (COVID-19) is no longer a public health emergency, it remains the most prevalent circulating infectious-like illness in Europe. Whether immunocompromising conditions (ICCs) still carry increased mortality risk during the Omicron era is unclear. METHODS: We conducted a cohort study across EuCARE sites in 8 countries among adults admitted to hospital with COVID-19 between 2020 and 2023. ICCs and COVID-19 pneumonia at hospitalization were defined using clinical information and ICD-10 codes. Logistic regression and counterfactual mediation analysis was used to compare 28-day in-hospital mortality risk associated with ICCs using COVID-19 pneumonia and vaccination at hospital entry as intermediates. Proportion of the total effect of ICCs mediated and the controlled direct effects (CDEs) were calculated. We also formally tested for interaction between SARS-CoV-2 variants and ICCs for mortality risk. RESULTS: A total of 42 488 individuals were included, of whom 1675 (3.9%) had an ICC. Fifty-five percent were male, and the median age was 67 (IQR, 52-79) years. Overall, 4344 (10.2%) individuals died in hospital. ICCs were associated with increased mortality (OR, 1.49 [95% CI, 1.25-1.79]), with no evidence for an attenuation during the Omicron phase (P value for interaction = .60). Mediation analyses showed that the total effect of ICCs was mediated by vaccination but only weakly by pneumonia. With Omicron, the excess mortality associated with ICC was higher under the scenario that everyone in the cohort was to develop COVID-19 pneumonia (CDE, 1.22 [95% CI, .09-1.65]). CONCLUSIONS: ICC remains a significant risk factor for in-hospital death, even during the Omicron era, particularly if the infection led to the development of pneumonia.

Question and background
The study aimed to investigate whether immunocompromising conditions (ICCs) still pose an increased mortality risk during the Omicron era of the COVID-19 pandemic. Although coronavirus disease 2019 (COVID-19) is no longer a public health emergency, it remains the most prevalent circulating infectious-like illness in Europe.
Design and population
A multinational cohort study was conducted across EuCARE sites in 8 countries among adults hospitalized with COVID-19 between 2020 and 2023. A total of 42,488 individuals were included, of whom 1,675 (3.9%) had an ICC.
Core methods
ICCs and COVID-19 pneumonia at hospitalization were defined using clinical information and ICD-10 codes. Logistic regression and counterfactual mediation analysis were used to compare 28-day in-hospital mortality risk associated with ICCs, using COVID-19 pneumonia and vaccination at hospital entry as intermediates. Proportion of the total effect of ICCs mediated and the controlled direct effects (CDEs) were calculated. The study also formally tested for interaction between SARS-CoV-2 variants and ICCs for mortality risk.
Main results
ICCs were associated with increased mortality (OR, 1.49 [95% CI, 1.25-1.79]). There was no evidence for an attenuation during the Omicron phase (P value for interaction = .60). Mediation analyses showed that the total effect of ICCs was mediated by vaccination but only weakly by pneumonia. With Omicron, the excess mortality associated with ICC was higher under the scenario that everyone in the cohort was to develop COVID-19 pneumonia (CDE, 1.22 [95% CI, .09-1.65]).
Interpretation and novelty
The authors concluded that ICC remains a significant risk factor for in-hospital death, even during the Omicron era, particularly if the infection led to the development of pneumonia. This finding highlights the persistent impact of immunocompromising conditions on mortality outcomes despite the emergence of the Omicron variant.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings suggest that immunocompromised individuals hospitalized with COVID-19 continue to face heightened mortality risks, even with the Omicron variant. This has important implications for targeted surveillance, diagnosis, and management strategies for immunocompromised patients during future outbreaks.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited by its reliance on clinical information and ICD-10 codes for defining ICCs and pneumonia, which may introduce bias. The evidence is moderate, as the study is based on an abstract-only analysis and does not provide detailed statistical or methodological information.

基于吲哚的拟肽马来酰亚胺作为 严重急性呼吸综合征冠状病毒2 Mpro 抑制剂支架,与可逆共价抑制兼容:弹头优化和广泛的体外 抗病毒药物 活性。

Indole-based peptidomimetic maleimides as SARS-CoV-2 Mpro inhibitor scaffolds compatible with reversible covalent inhibition: Warhead optimization and broad in vitro antiviral activity.
作者： Pengyao Jin, Yangjie Zeng, Zhida Fan, Mengguang Liu, Likang Zhai, Rugui Xiao, Yali Zhang, Xiaodong Ren
摘要中文翻译尽管几种严重急性呼吸综合征冠状病毒2主要蛋白酶(Mpro)抑制剂已获得临床授权,但进化的持续使用和耐药相关突变的出现凸显了对具有平衡效力、化学稳定性和可逆共价反应性的额外抑制剂支架的需求。在此,我们报告了基于吲哚的拟肽马来酰亚胺作为 Mpro 抑制剂支架的发现和弹头优化,其结合模式与可逆共价抑制兼容。最初尝试获取 α-氰基丙烯酸酯类似物,得到了与产品相关的馏分,其色谱和质谱图在纯化和分析处理过程中发生了变化,从而阻碍了分离分析纯样品以进行生物分析。DFT 计算显示 α-氰基丙烯酸酯模型具有更高的亲电性,并为两类弹头的净化和处理行为的对比提供了支持性的电子原理。随后对马来酰亚胺系列进行优化,确定化合物 8i 作为主要抑制剂,对野生型 Mpro 具有个位数纳摩尔抑制作用(IC50 = 6.51 nM)。跳跃稀释实验支持与可逆共价抑制模式兼容的可逆结合成分。在补充 P-gp 抑制剂的 Vero E6 CPE 测定中,8i 对野生型、Delta 和 Omicron BA.5 严重急性呼吸综合征冠状病毒2 变体表现出广泛的体外 抗病毒药物 活性,EC50 值分别为 1.44、0.49 和 1.52 μM,而在应用测定中浓度高达 100 μM 时未达到 50% 细胞毒性条件。使用共价对接和 100 ns 分子动力学模拟为建模的 Mpro 复合物提供支持性结构解释,并将化合物 8i 与活性较低的 N-取代类似物 8g 进行比较。由于针对所选突变蛋白酶的生化活性尚未通过实验确定,因此对接结果应解释为假设生成的结构模型,而不是 突变 耐受性的证据。总体而言,这些发现将基于吲哚的拟肽马来酰亚胺确定为化学上可处理的 Mpro 抑制剂支架,其结合模式与可逆共价抑制兼容,并支持化合物 8i 作为进一步 抗病毒药物 优化的先导。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
由于严重急性呼吸综合征冠状病毒2的持续进化和耐药相关突变的出现,需要进行这项研究,这突出表明需要具有平衡效力、化学稳定性和可逆共价反应性的额外抑制剂支架。
设计与对象
该研究报告了基于吲哚的拟肽马来酰亚胺作为 Mpro 抑制剂支架的发现和弹头优化,其结合模式与可逆共价抑制兼容。
核心方法
获取 α-氰基丙烯酸酯类似物的初步尝试导致了与产品相关的馏分,其色谱和质谱图谱在纯化和分析处理过程中发生了变化,从而阻碍了分离分析纯样品以进行生物分析。
主要结果
DFT 计算显示 α-氰基丙烯酸酯模型具有更高的亲电性,并为两类弹头的净化和处理行为的对比提供了支持性的电子原理。
解释与创新
随后对马来酰亚胺系列进行优化,确定化合物 8i 作为主要抑制剂,对野生型 Mpro 具有个位数纳摩尔抑制作用(IC50 = 6.51 nM)。
科研与公卫意义
在补充 P-gp 抑制剂的 Vero E6 CPE 测定中,8i 对野生型、Delta 和 Omicron BA.5 严重急性呼吸综合征冠状病毒2 变体表现出广泛的体外 抗病毒药物 活性,EC50 值分别为 1.44、0.49 和 1.52 μM,而在应用测定中浓度高达 100 μM 时未达到 50% 细胞毒性条件。
局限与证据强度
由于针对所选突变蛋白酶的生化活性尚未通过实验确定,因此对接结果应解释为假设生成的结构模型,而不是 突变 耐受性的证据。

Indole-based peptidomimetic maleimides as SARS-CoV-2 Mpro inhibitor scaffolds compatible with reversible covalent inhibition: Warhead optimization and broad in vitro antiviral activity.

Authors: Pengyao Jin, Yangjie Zeng, Zhida Fan, Mengguang Liu, Likang Zhai, Rugui Xiao, Yali Zhang, Xiaodong Ren
Original abstract (English)
Although several SARS-CoV-2 main protease (Mpro) inhibitors have been clinically authorized, the continued evolution of SARS-CoV-2 and the emergence of resistance-associated mutations highlight the need for additional inhibitor scaffolds with balanced potency, chemical stability, and reversible covalent reactivity. Herein, we report the discovery and warhead optimization of indole-based peptidomimetic maleimides as Mpro inhibitor scaffolds with a binding mode compatible with reversible covalent inhibition. Initial attempts to access α-cyanoacrylate analogues afforded product-related fractions whose chromatographic and mass-spectrometric profiles changed during purification and analytical handling, preventing isolation of analytically pure samples for biological profiling. DFT calculations showed higher electrophilicity for the α-cyanoacrylate model and provided a supportive electronic rationale for the contrasting purification and handling behavior of the two warhead classes. Subsequent optimization of the maleimide series identified compound 8i as the lead inhibitor, with single-digit nanomolar inhibition of wild-type Mpro (IC50 = 6.51 nM). Jump-dilution experiments supported a reversible binding component compatible with a reversible covalent inhibition mode. In P-gp inhibitor-supplemented Vero E6 CPE assays, 8i showed broad in vitro antiviral activity against wild-type, Delta, and Omicron BA.5 SARS-CoV-2 variants, with EC50 values of 1.44, 0.49, and 1.52 μM, respectively, while 50% cytotoxicity was not reached at concentrations up to 100 μM under the applied assay conditions. Covalent docking and 100 ns molecular dynamics simulations were used to provide supportive structural interpretations of the modeled Mpro complexes and to compare compound 8i with the less active N-substituted analogue 8g. Because biochemical activity against the selected mutant proteases has not been experimentally determined, the docking results should be interpreted as hypothesis-generating structural models rather than evidence of mutation tolerance. Overall, these findings identify indole-based peptidomimetic maleimides as chemically tractable Mpro inhibitor scaffolds with a binding mode compatible with reversible covalent inhibition and support compound 8i as a lead for further antiviral optimization.

Changes in Dengue Epidemiology in China Before, During, and After COVID-19 2015-2024: A Retrospective National Surveillance and Spatiotemporal Study.

Changes in Dengue Epidemiology in China Before, During, and After COVID-19 2015-2024: A Retrospective National Surveillance and Spatiotemporal Study.
作者： Yu Chen, Meng Shang, Sheng-Ping Dou, Xiao-Xu Wang, Hao-Qiang Ji, Qi-Yong Liu
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
登革热仍然是中国主要的蚊媒疾病。然而,关于其长期流行病学影响的全国范围内的综合证据有限。
设计与对象
我们利用 2015 年至 2024 年中国报告的登革热病例进行了一项全国范围的回顾性时空研究。比较了大流行前、大流行和大流行后阶段的流行病学特征。
核心方法
Morlet 小波分析用于评估时间周期性。适合大流行前数据的 SARIMA 模型生成了 2020-2024 年的反事实预测。使用省级 发病率 制图、全球 Moran's I 和局部空间关联指标来评估空间模式。
主要结果
总共报告了 87 182 例登革热病例,主要高峰出现在 2019 年和 2024 年。输入病例在 2020-2022 年期间急剧下降,并在 2023 年之后反弹,而本土病例 传播 日益占主导地位。年度周期性在疫情期间减弱,但在2023年之后重新出现。2020-2022年观察到的发病率经常低于反事实预期,而自2023年中期出现大幅正偏差,并在2024年加剧。疫情后登革热风险在华南和西南地区重新出现,并向内陆扩展,省际聚集性较弱。
解释与创新
COVID-19 and related NPIs substantially reshaped dengue epidemiology in China, suppressing transmission during 2020-2022 but followed by a marked post-pandemic resurgence, increasing indigenous transmission, disrupted but re-emerging seasonality, and broader inland spread.
科研与公卫意义
研究结果可以谨慎地为中国登革热的传播策略、风险评估和公共卫生干预措施提供参考,特别是在了解大流行相关措施和登革热传播动态之间的相互作用方面。
局限与证据强度
该研究基于报告的登革热病例,并未考虑未报告或诊断不足的病例。证据是中等的,因为它依赖于回顾性分析和统计模型。

Changes in Dengue Epidemiology in China Before, During, and After COVID-19 2015-2024: A Retrospective National Surveillance and Spatiotemporal Study.

Authors: Yu Chen, Meng Shang, Sheng-Ping Dou, Xiao-Xu Wang, Hao-Qiang Ji, Qi-Yong Liu
Original abstract (English)
BACKGROUND: Dengue remains a major mosquito-borne disease in China. The COVID-19 pandemic and associated non-pharmaceutical interventions (NPIs) profoundly altered human mobility and public health responses, potentially reshaping dengue transmission dynamics. However, comprehensive nationwide evidence on their long-term epidemiological impact is limited. OBJECTIVE: This study aimed to evaluate changes in dengue epidemiology in China before, during, and after the COVID-19 pandemic, with a focus on temporal periodicity, importation and indigenous transmission, counterfactual deviations from pre-pandemic trends, and spatial risk redistribution. METHODS: We conducted a nationwide retrospective spatiotemporal study using reported dengue cases in China from 2015 to 2024. Epidemiological characteristics were compared across pre-pandemic, pandemic, and post-pandemic phases. Morlet wavelet analysis was used to assess temporal periodicity. A SARIMA model fitted to pre-pandemic data generated counterfactual forecasts for 2020-2024. Spatial patterns were evaluated using provincial incidence mapping, Global Moran's I, and Local Indicators of Spatial Association. RESULTS: A total of 87 182 dengue cases were reported, with major peaks in 2019 and 2024. Imported cases declined sharply during 2020-2022 and rebounded after 2023, whereas indigenous transmission became increasingly dominant. Annual periodicity weakened during the pandemic but re-emerged after 2023. Observed incidence during 2020-2022 was frequently below counterfactual expectations, while substantial positive deviations appeared from mid-2023 and intensified in 2024. Post-pandemic dengue risk re-emerged in South and Southwest China and expanded inland, with weaker provincial clustering. CONCLUSIONS: COVID-19 and related NPIs substantially reshaped dengue epidemiology in China, suppressing transmission during 2020-2022 but followed by a marked post-pandemic resurgence, increasing indigenous transmission, disrupted but re-emerging seasonality, and broader inland spread.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Dengue remains a major mosquito-borne disease in China. However, comprehensive nationwide evidence on their long-term epidemiological impact is limited.
Design and population
We conducted a nationwide retrospective spatiotemporal study using reported dengue cases in China from 2015 to 2024. Epidemiological characteristics were compared across pre-pandemic, pandemic, and post-pandemic phases.
Core methods
Morlet wavelet analysis was used to assess temporal periodicity. A SARIMA model fitted to pre-pandemic data generated counterfactual forecasts for 2020-2024. Spatial patterns were evaluated using provincial incidence mapping, Global Moran's I, and Local Indicators of Spatial Association.
Main results
A total of 87 182 dengue cases were reported, with major peaks in 2019 and 2024. Importing cases declined sharply during 2020-2022 and rebounded after 2023, whereas indigenous transmission became increasingly dominant. Annual periodicity weakened during the pandemic but re-emerged after 2023. Observed incidence during 2020-2022 was frequently below counterfactual expectations, while substantial positive deviations appeared from mid-2023 and intensified in 2024. Post-pandemic dengue risk re-emerged in South and Southwest China and expanded inland, with weaker provincial clustering.
Interpretation and novelty
COVID-19 and related NPIs substantially reshaped dengue epidemiology in China, suppressing transmission during 2020-2022 but followed by a marked post-pandemic resurgence, increasing indigenous transmission, disrupted but re-emerging seasonality, and broader inland spread.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings can cautiously inform surveillance strategies, risk assessment, and public health interventions for dengue in China, particularly in understanding the interplay between pandemic-related measures and dengue transmission dynamics.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is based on reported dengue cases and does not account for unreported or underdiagnosed cases. The evidence is moderate as it relies on retrospective analysis and statistical modeling.
